### Lines shared with other tablets
- **VII 28–29 = VI 128–129** (two-line speech formula): `אנקידו פתח את פיו ואמר, / דיבר אל גילגמש:`, here with VII's own brackets (`אֶנְקִידוּ [פתח] את פ[יו ואמר],` / `דיבר [אל גִּלְגָּמֶשׁ]:`).
- **VII 44–45 → V 316–317** (not yet translated; first copy here): `שישה קנים גּוֹבְהֵךְ, שני קנים רוֹחְבֵּךְ, [אמה] אחת [עוֹבְיֵךְ],` / `עמודך, צירך ו*פּוֹתֵךְ* – מאחד הם.` In V the pronouns are 3rd person (*mēlûša*, *rupussu*, *šukûša* …), V 316 begins with *ēpuš-ma dalta* "I made a door", and *ištēt ammat uppuša* "one cubit its thickness" stands in V 317: V should copy the Hebrew word for word and change only that.
- **VII 65–67, 92**: the tears formula (`מהר, חיש, זולגות דמעותיו`; `זולגות דמעותיו` as VI 83). **VII 68**: the one-line speech formula. **VII 90**: the dawn formula (`כשהאיר אור הבוקר`). **VII 148**: `שמע X את דבר Y`. **VII 165 ~ I 244**: `החלום אשר ראיתי בלילה הזה`, with *mimmû* `מה` for *ummī*. **VII 255**: `יום אחד … יום שני`.
- **VII 91–92 ~ VI 82–83, V 90–91, 185–186**: `לפני X … זולגות דמעותיו/דמעותיה`; V should use `נשא גילגמש את ראשו, לפני שמש בוכה,` / `לפני קרני שַׁמַשׁ זולגות דמעותיו:` (V 90–91 have *ibakki*, VII *unabbi* `מילל`).
- **VII 94 = I 111** + *šū*: `[הוא?] – צייד, אדם לוכד,`.
- **VII 102 = 151** (brackets only).
- **VII 132 → IV 194** (`שמש שמע את מאמר פיו,`), **VII 133 → V 96** (`מיד – תרועה מן השמים צועקת וצועקת אליו:`; V's own brackets).
- **VII 135–136 ~ VI 27–28**: `לחם הראוי לאֵלוּת`, `סובא מובחר … הראוי למלכות`, as fixed in VI.
- **VII 139–147 → VIII 84–91** (first copy here; VIII in the 1st person): VIII 84 `אשכיב אותך במשכב גדול,`, 85 `במשכב של חיבה אשכיב אותך,`, 86 `אושיב אותך במשכן מנוחה, משכן השמאל,`, 87 `נסיכי הקרקע ינשקו את רגליך,`, 88 `אַבְכֶּה עליך את אנשי אורוך, אַאֲבִיל אותם עליך,`, 89 `את האנשים השופעים אמלא עליך עמל.`, 90 `ואני – אחריך אַשִּׂיא את גוויתי פרע,`, 91 `לבשתי עור לביא, ומשוטט אני בשדה.` (VIII 91 *altabbiš … arappud*; cf. X 62 `וּמְשׁוֹטֵט אֲנִי בַּשָּׂדֶה`).
- **VII 147 ~ X 45** (fixed passage §3.2): `ומשוטט … בשדה`.
- **VII 182 ~ VI 61, 76**: *târu* D = `הפך`.
- **VII 184–192 → Ištar's Descent 4–11, Nergal and Ereškigal c+1–c+7** (not in this corpus): the Hebrew fixed here should bind a future translation of those texts.
- **VII 193 = 198** (brackets only).
- **VII 251 ~ X 55–56, 132–133, 232–233; OB VA+BM**: `אשר עמך עברתי את כל התלאות` (1st person of the fixed passage).
- **VII 256 ~ XI 245** (Jensen's restoration *ašib mūtu*, not translated).
- `verify.py` reports no differences: eBL's exact parallels for VII point only to the MB/Hittite and other-text chapters.

### Proposals for the shared renderings
1. **Key terms (§4):** *arāru* infinitive = `לְאָרֵר` (Piel; the §4 row's `לָאֹר` is a plene homograph of "to the light"); confirm that VII 101 is the only *arāru* of VII (all other curses *nazāru*/*ezēru* = `גִּדֵּף`). *zêru* "hate" = `שָׂנֵא` (VII 61, 263; eBL lemmatizes *ezēru*). *ezēbu* = `עָזַב` (+ N `נֶעֱזַב`) as a key verb (VII 75–76, 161 ↔ Gen 2:24). *eṣēru* D stative = `צוּיְּרוּ` (VII 88). *imtu* = `חֵמָה` [cognate] (VII 175). *ēdēnu* = `לְבַד` (VII 163 ↔ Gen 2:18). *bīt eklēti* = `בֵּית הַחֲשֵׁכָה`; *bīt epri* = `בֵּית הֶעָפָר`; *tayyāru* = `שָׁב` (root *târu*); *ēribu* = `בָּא` (`בָּאָיו`); *bubūtu* = `מָזוֹן`. *baltu* / *ašāgu* = `חוֹחַ` / `חֵדֶק` (never `קוֹץ` / `דַּרְדַּר`); *paḫāru* = `קַדָּר` (never `יוֹצֵר`). *uznu* "ear/understanding" note at every occurrence. *labāšu* D = `הִלְבִּישׁ` (VII 137) added to the Gen 3:21 rows.
2. **Grammar:** Gtn iteratives never with `שוב ושוב` (the root of *târu*): `הִקְשִׁיב` (*šemû* Gtn), doubling (`צועקת וצועקת`), or `פעם אחר פעם`; align VI 84 (open decision 2).
3. **Collision to fix in Tablet I:** I 57 renders *uqnâti* `[תְּכֵלֶת]`, while VII 127 has *takiltu*, the real cognate of `תְּכֵלֶת`; I 57 should become `[לזורית]` (as `../renderings.md` §4 *uqnû* = `לָזוּרִית`).
4. **Ordinary words (§5):** *daltu* `דֶּלֶת` (already); *ḫalbu* = `חֹרֶשׁ`; *nasāqu* = `בָּרַר`; *nindanu* = `קָנֶה`; *šukû* = `עַמּוּד`; *sāḫiru* = `צִיר`; *šagammu* = `*פּוֹת*`; *retû* = `קָבַע`; *amu* = `רַפְסוֹדָה`; *zaqāpu* = `זָקַף`; *nērebu* = `מָבוֹא`; *gimillu* = `גְּמוּל`; *šūquru* = `יְקַר־עֵרֶךְ`, *aqru* = `יָקָר`; *pirittu* = `בְּעָתָה`; *zubbu* = `זְבוּב`; *ḫawû* = `הָמָה`; *ṣullû* = `הִתְחַנֵּן`, *suppû* = `הִפְצִיר`; *utnennu* = `תְּחִנָּה`; *nubbû* = `יִלֵּל`; *deššu* = `יָתֵד`; *šarūru* = `קֶרֶן (אוֹר)`; *nēmelu* = `רֶוַח`; *idū* = `מַשְׂכֹּרֶת`; *ḫalāqu* D = `אִבֵּד`; *zittu* = `חֵלֶק`; *ḫarṣu* = `גָּרוּעַ`; *apāniš* = `בַּחַלּוֹן`; *dinnû* = `יָצוּעַ`; *dakkannu* = `*סַפְסָל*`; *išpallurtu* = `פָּרָשַׁת (דְּרָכִים)`; *ḫurbu* = `חָרְבָּה`; *maṣallu* = `מְלוּנָה`; *manzāzu* = `מַעֲמָד`; *qalāpu* = `קָלַף`; *ṣamû* = `צָמֵא`; *šakru* = `שִׁכּוֹר`; *tarbuʾu* = `אָבָק`; *bēlet dīni* = `בַּעֲלַת דִּין`; *sêru* = `טָח`; *itinnu* = `בַּנַּאי`; *ūru* "roof" = `גַּג`; *qadû* = `יַנְשׁוּף`; *rabāṣu* = `רָבַץ`; *qerītu* = `מִשְׁתֶּה`; *takiltu* = `תְּכֵלֶת`; *sūnu* = `חֲלָצַיִם`; *sehû* D = `חִלֵּל`; *tukku* = `תְּרוּעָה`; *teknû* = `חִבָּה`; *nēḫtu* = `מְנוּחָה`; *malku* = `נָסִיךְ`; *bakû* Š = `הִבְכָּה`; *damāmu* Š = `הֶאֱבִיל`; *našû* Š = `הִשִּׂיא`; *aggu* = `קוֹצֵף`, *ezzu* = `זוֹעֵם`; *šakkanakku* = `מוֹשֵׁל`; *rēdû* = `חַיָּל`; *miserru* = `מֵזַח`; *paṭāru* = `הִתִּיר`; *ṣurru* = `צוֹר`; *inṣabtu* = `נֶזֶם`; *nidinnu* = `מַתָּנָה`; *ganūnu* = `מַחְסָן`; *išpiku* = `אָסָם`; *šapku* = `שָׁפוּךְ`; *wabālu* Št = `הִרְהֵר`; *ukkulu* = `מֻחְשָׁךְ`; *rittu* = `כַּף`; *ṣupru* = `צִפֹּרֶן`; *danānu* D = `חָזַק עַל`; *keppû* = `חֶבֶל־קְפִיצָה`; *ṭebû* D = `הִטְבִּיעַ`; *raḫāṣu* I = `רָמַס`; *šabāḫu* = `שָׁקַע`; *kamāsu* I = `כִּנֵּס`; *bêlu* = `מָשַׁל`; *šumû* = `צְלִי`; *ultu ūmē pāna* = `מִימֵי קֶדֶם`; *ṭupšarru(tu)* = `סוֹפֵר(ת)`; *kimaḫḫu* = `קֶבֶר`; *murṣu* = `חֳלִי`; *gamāru* = `גָּמַר`; *qerbu* "midst" = `תּוֹךְ` beside *qablu* = `קְרָב`.
5. **Names (§1):** Irkalla `אִירְכַּלָּה` used as both place and goddess (note); priest titles transliterated with `כּוֹהֵן` (`כּוֹהֲנֵי־אֵן`, `־לַגָּר`, `־לוּמַחּוּ`, `־גוּדַפְּסוּ`; *išippu* `כּוֹהֵן־טָהֳרָה`).

### Lessons for the guidelines
- **A reserved root can enter through an adverb**: the Gtn iterative rendered `שוב ושוב` puts the root of *târu* (Gen 3:19 `עַד שׁוּבְךָ`) into lines that have no *târu*; grep reserved roots in adverbs and idioms too, not only in verbs.
- **One eBL lemma can cover three verbs** (*ezēru* for *nazāru* "curse", *ezēru* proper and *zêru* "hate"): parse the form (hollow vs strong root) before deciding which Hebrew verb – and therefore which Gen 3 contact – a line gets.
- **A coordinator's speech grouping should be checked against the section breaks and the content** (VII 84–89 is Enkidu's reply, not Gilgameš's answer); record the attribution as an open decision when no formula survives.
- **Stock passages shared with other compositions** (VII 184–192 = Ištar's Descent 4–11) should be fixed once, in the first translated copy, and listed for the corpus that will translate the other text.

## Tablet X

### Scope
SB Tablet X, all 325 eBL labels (1–294, 292′–294′, 295–322): Šiduri the ale-wife at the sea-shore (1–4); Gilgameš arrives in a pelt, she bars the gate and goes up on the roof (5–18); the threat to the door and the exchange (19–39); the question and the lament, first copy (40–71); "what is the road to Ūta-napišti?" – no crossing, only Šamaš, the Waters of Death, Ur-šanabi and the Stone Ones (72–91); Gilgameš smashes the Stone Ones (92–112); the question and the lament, second copy (113–148); the punting-poles (149–172); the crossing of the Waters of Death, twelve poles, the garment as sail (173–183); Ūta-napišti sees the boat, "Long live Ūta-napišti" (184–211); the third copy (212–248); Gilgameš's account of his wanderings and the gate of sorrow (249–265); Ūta-napišti's speech: the fool's lot, the king's duties, the moon and the wakeful gods (266–294′), and the speech on death (295–322). Manuscripts: Nineveh NinNA1a (K₁), NinNA1c (K₃), K 8579 (K₂); Kalḫu KalNA1 (z); Babylon BabNB1 (f, + BM 36257) and the Parthian BabPar2 (b, + BM 34306); l. 1 also as the IX catch-line (NinNAQuo1). No manuscript at all for 47–60, 126–130 (and the copies are fully broken in 61–62, 136, 139, 142, 146, 148, 234, 238); single-manuscript stretches 47–211 (NinNA1a, KalNA1 for 196–211); 280–294 BabPar2 only.

### Sources consulted
- eBL `L/1/4` SB X (`../../sources/eBL/Gilgamesh-SB-X/reading-edition.md`: N, T, L, notes, parallels, ATF; `manuscripts.md`).
- eBL OB VA+BM (`../../sources/eBL/Gilgamesh-OB-VA-BM/reading-edition.md`) for the OB parallels (not translated; cited in the apparatus).
- `../renderings.md` incl. §9–§11 (binding; §3.2 copied in plene spelling), Tablets I, VI, VII and XI 209–328 (`annotated-*.rtl.md`), flood XI 1–208 and its renderings (*peḫû* = `סָתַם`, *kupru* = `כֹּפֶר`, *iṭṭû* = `חֵמָר`, *parīsu*), creation corpus renderings (*mê mūti*, *ḫepû* = `בִּקַּע`, *awû* = `שָׂח`, *sakātu*), law corpus (*maḫāṣu*, *kīma* = `כְּ`, *bēlu* = `בַּעַל`).
- `../parallels_for.py X --all` (177 lines with parallels).
- George's translation (eBL T), after drafting; Tchernichovsky 1924 Tablet X (`../../sources/Ben-Yehuda--Tchernichovsky-1924/full-work--all-tablets.rtl.md`, ll. 2827–3409) after pass (c).
- The vocalized Bible through `../../tools/check_bible_quotes.py --show`.

### Decisions
1. **The files are generated from one data file** (scratchpad `X-part*.txt`: Hebrew, apparatus and footnote per label; the normalization is filled in from eBL by script), so the annotated `>` lines and the clean translation are identical by construction (guidelines §5, "generate … from one line-by-line data file").
2. ***sābītu* = `בַּעֲלַת־הַמִּסְבָּאָה`** (vocalized at 1), always with the maqaf, also in the vocative (73, 258). Reason: *sābītu* is from *sabû* "brew/sell beer" ~ Hebrew סבא (`סֹבֶא`, already *sīrāšu* in `../renderings.md` §4; `זוֹלֵל וְסֹבֵא`); the later `מִסְבָּאָה` keeps the root and the trade. Rejected: `הַמּוֹזֶגֶת` (hides the root), `הַסּוֹבְאָה` ("drunkard"), `פּוּנְדְּקָאִית` (OB: she brews and sells beer).
3. **The fixed passage §3.2 (40–71, 113–148, 213–248) is copied in plene spelling**, as every `>` line: the table's vocalized forms stripped by the strip-test give `זיווך`, `זיווי`, `פניי`, `עליי`, `במעיי`, `מאוד`, `שישה`, `המוות`, `באורח`, `אשתוק`, `אֶדּוֹם` (vocalized: unvocalized "אדום" = red), `שׂוּמִים` (vocalized: "שומים" = garlic) – the only differences from the table's consonants (checked by script). Each copy brackets by its own manuscripts; 47–60 and 126–130 are fully bracketed (no manuscript of that copy). `(למה)` of 42 is kept in all copies. Quotation marks open at 47, 120, 220 and close at 71, 148, 248.
4. **First copies fixed here** (bind later tablets): 31–34 / 128–131 / 228–231 → VIII 52–54 (`אשר נסמכנו (יחד) ועלינו אל ההר,` / `שור השמים אחזנו, ושור השמים קטלנו,` / `השמדנו את חומבבה, אשר ביער הארזים יושב,` / `במבואות ההרים הרגנו אריות.`); 53–54 → VIII 50–51; 62 → IX 5 (`פחדתי מן המוות, ומשוטט אני בשדה.`); 93–94 → IX 15–16 (`נשא גרזן בזרועו,` / `שלף חרב מחגורו,`); 96 → IX 17 (`כחץ אל ביניהם נפל,`); 171 → IV 4, 37, 82, 123 (`מהלך ירח וחצי – ביום השלישי …`); 259–260 → VIII 16–17 (animal names).
5. ***matīma* = `אִי־פַּעַם`** (79, 270), because `מֵעוֹלָם` is reserved; ***im-matīma … im-matīma*** (308–312) = `פעם – … / פעם – …` ("sometimes … sometimes"), which keeps the list-of-times structure (↔ Qoh 3).
6. **The Waters of Death and the crossing:** *mê mūti* = `מֵי הַמָּוֶת` (creation corpus); *šūt abnī* = `אַנְשֵׁי הָאֶבֶן` (§1); *ḫepû* D = `בִּקַּע`, *ḫuppû* = `מְבֻקָּעִים` (creation corpus); *tabāku* = `יָצַק` (flood shared, against XI 316's `נָטַשׁ` "by sense": here the stones are "poured" into the river); *peḫû* = `סָתַם` (flood XI); *nēberu* = `מַעְבָּר`, *nēbertu* = `מַעְבָּרָה`; *pašāqu* G/Š = `צָרָה` / `מוּצֶרֶת`; *parāku* = `חוֹסֵם`; *parīsu* = `מוֹט` (XI 276; flood XI 65 has `מוט־דחיפה`); *nindanu* = `קָנֶה`; *kapāru* "trim" = `קִצֵּעַ` (not `קָלַף` = *qalāpu*; no link with כפר / *kupru*); *tulû* "boss" = `דַּד`; *lapātu* = `נָגַע` (175 ↔ Gen 3:3); *magillu* = `סִירָה`, *elippu* = `סְפִינָה`; *karû* (183) = `תּוֹרֶן` (eBL's lemma "grain heap" does not fit; `כְּרִי` kept for the grain heap); *kappu* = `כָּנָף` also for the arms (101, 183).
7. **Gilgameš's equipment:** *namṣaru* = `חֶרֶב` (§5); *šibbu* "sword-belt" = `חֲגוֹר` (2 Sam 20:8 `חֲגוֹר חֶרֶב`) – a different word from Gen 3:7 `חֲגֹרֹת`, but the root חגר is also *ezēḫu* (I): see proposals. *šalāpu* = `שָׁלַף` [cognate]; *ḫalālu* = `הִתְגַּנֵּב`; *šiltāḫu* = `חֵץ`; *namrīru* = `נֹגַהּ`; *raʾābu* = `רָעַד`, *šâbu* = `רָטַט`.
8. **Šiduri's house:** *kannu* = `כַּן` [cognate] (`כִּיּוֹר … וְכַנּוֹ`, Exod 30:18; vocalized `כַּנִּים`); *kutummu kuttumu* = `בכיסויים מכוסה`; the restored *kulūlu kullulu* = `[בעטיפות? עטופה?]` – not `צָעִיף` (Gen 24:65; 38:14), which would put a Genesis word in a lacuna; *edēlu* = `נָעַל`; *ūru* "roof" = `גַּג` (VII); *nagāšu* Gtn = `תָּעָה` (rapādu has `שׁוֹטֵט`, `נָד` is reserved); *sapannu* = `שְׁפֵלָה`.
9. **Speech and thought:** *uštamma ana libbīša amāta iqabbi* = `שׂחה אל לבה, דבר אומרת,` (11; 185 with *izzakkar* = `דבר מדבר`), *awû* Št = `שָׂח` (creation corpus); *malāku* Gt = `נִמְלַךְ`; *ramānu* = `עַצְמִי`. *mindē-ma* = `אולי` (CDA), against George's "for sure" (13).
10. **The road and its sign:** *ḫarrānu* = `אֹרַח` (73, 150: `מהו אורח אותנפישתי?`), *ittu* = `אוֹת` (74–75, 151–152: `האות שלה`, not `אותה`, which reads "her"). 151 *idna* (NinNA1a writes *id-ni*, the feminine of 74): masculine `תן`. The formula *šumma naṭū-ma / šumma lā naṭū-ma* = `אם ראוי – … / ואם לא ראוי – …` (§2) with *lūbir* = `אעבורה`, *lurpud* = `אשוטטה`, *ebir* = `עבור`, *iḫis* = `הִסּוֹג` (*naḫāsu* = `נָסוֹג`, §10).
11. **Šamaš and the sun:** `שַׁמַשׁ` (god, 81–82) and `שֶׁמֶשׁ` (*šamšu*, the sun, 314) vocalized every time (both occur, `../renderings.md` §1). *qurādu* = `גִּבּוֹר` (↔ Ps 19:6).
12. **The wanderings (249–265):** *umma* = `(אמרתי) כך:` with the inner quotation in `'…'`; *saḫāru* = `חָזַר` (`חזרתי, הלכתי`, "go around" – George "again"); *etēqu* Gtn = `חלפתי פעם אחר פעם`, *ebēru* Gtn = `עברתי פעם אחר פעם` (no `שוב ושוב`, §11); *šittu ṭābtu* = `שינה טובה` (`טוב` for *ṭābu*); *nazāqu* Š (eBL: *esēqu*) = `ייסרתי את עצמי`; *dalāpu* = `עֵר`, *ina dalāpi* = `בְּעֵרוּת` (vocalized; "בבערות"), *taddalip* = `עַרְתָּ`; *šerʾānu* = `גִּיד`; *kašāru* Gt = `השיג`; *mānaḫtu* = `יְגִיעַ`; *ana lēt* = `אל צד` (by sense; *lētu* = `לְחִי` elsewhere); *lubuštu* = `לְבוּשׁ`; *mindinu* = `*בַּרְדְּלָס*`; *ṭabāḫu* D "flay" = `הִפְשִׁיט` (Lev 1:6); *kupru u ittû* = `בכּוֹפֶר ובחֵמָר` (vocalized `כּוֹפֶר`, plene of `כֹּפֶר`); *mēlulu* = `מָחוֹל`; *ḫaddû* = `שָׂמֵחַ` (I 232).
13. **Ūta-napišti's speech (266–322):** *ballu* (*balālu*) = `בָּלוּל`; *lillu* = `כְּסִיל`; *nadû* (a throne) = `השליכו` (literal, as I 100, VII 88); *puḫru* = `אֲסֵפָה`; *šuršummu* = `שְׁמָרִים`; *ḫimētu* = `חֶמְאָה` [cognate]; *gunnu* = `*גס*`; *tuḫḫu* = `סובין`, *kukkušu* = `*קמח גרוע*`; *mašḫantu* = `*סחבה*`; *nēbeḫu* = `אַבְנֵט`, *ebēḫu* = `חָגַר`; *milku* = `עֵצָה`; *rēša našû* = `נשא ראש` (↔ Gen 40:13); *ēru lā sākipu* = `ער, לא נם`; *ultu pān šakin* = `מִלְּפָנִים שׂוּם`; *kapādu* = `חֲשׁוֹב`; *tappûtu* = `חֲבֵרוּת`; *zāninu* = `מְכַלְכֵּל` (↔ Gen 45:11; 47:12); *ištarātu* = `הָאֵלוֹת` (vocalized: "האלות" = the curses); *qīštu* = `תְּשׁוּרָה` (apart from *nidinnu* = `מַתָּנָה`); *qerēbu* D = `קֵרֵב` (300 ↔ Gen 47:29); *qanû* = `קָנֶה`, *apu* = `אֲגַם`; *ḫaṣābu* / *ḫāṣipu* = `נגדע` / `גודע` (301, 307; George 2003 reads one verb, eBL lemmatizes 307 as *ḫasāpu*); *šumu* = `שֵׁם` (George "progeny", noted); *šalālu* = `שָׁלַל`, *šallu* = `שָׁלוּל`; *aggu* = `קוֹצֵף`; *bītu* … *epēšu* = `עושים בית`; *qinna qanānu* = `מקננים קן` (`../renderings.md` §4, cognate row); *zâzu* = `חָלַק`; *zērūtu* = `שִׂנְאָה`; *mīlu* = `גֵּאוּת` (not the deluge); *našû* intransitive = `נִשָּׂא`; *kulīlu* = `שַׁפִּירִית`; *neqelpû* = `צָף`; *eṣēru* = `צָר` with *ṣalmu* = `צֶלֶם` (317, as I 49); *lullû* = `קַדְמוֹן` (318); *karābu* = `בֵּרֵךְ` "greet"; *paḫāru* G = `הִתְקַבֵּץ`; *bānât šīmti* = `בּוֹנַת הַגּוֹרָל` (*banû* = `בָּנָה`, never `בָּרָא` / `יוֹצֶרֶת`); *šīmta šâmu* = `גורל גזרה`; *šakānu* = `שׂמו` (321); *uddû* = `הוֹדִיעוּ` (322, *edû* D = `ידע`), *ūmīšu* plural = `ימיו` (George "the day").
14. **Names** vocalized at first occurrence in the tablet: שִׁידוּרִי (1), גִּלְגָּמֶשׁ (5), אֶנְקִידוּ (30), חוּמְבָּבָה (33, restored), אוּתְנַפִּשְׁתִּי (73), שַׁמַשׁ (81–82), אוּרְשַׁנַבִּי (87), אוּבָּר־טוּטוּ (208), אָנוּ (267), סִין (281), אֲנוּנָּכִּי (319), מַמִּיתוּם (320; unvocalized it reads like "killing them"). `רֵעִי` always vocalized; `אֵלִים` vocalized without the article (7, 268); `שֵׁב` (271) vocalized (unvocalized "שב" = "returned", the root of *târu*).
15. **Speech marks follow the speakers, not George's quotation marks**: no new quotation at 257, 274, 286 (George's T opens or closes there inside one speech); Ūta-napišti's speech 266–293 is closed at 293 and reopened at 293′ (as George), running to 322.
16. **eBL slips recorded in the apparatus:** 35 N *ana šâši* for the manuscript's *ana šâšu* (to him); 157 *tuḫtappi* lemmatized as the adjective *ḫepû* I; 176–179 ordinals lemmatized as cardinals and other words ("fifty", "one-sixth", "eighty", "ninety", "20th day"); 183 *karâ* as *karû* "grain heap"; 216, 223 *arḫi* as *warḫu* "month" and *mašlū* as *mašlu* "half"; 255 *uštēziq* as *esēqu*; 259 *asa* as *asu* "myrtle"; 263 *kupri* as *gupru* "shepherd's hut"; 278 *īši* as *išû* (probably the imperative *iši*); 293 T "throw down" against the lemma *nadānu*; *ṣēru* "back" throughout. eBL's parallels field pairs many lines of the threefold refrain with the wrong neighbour (e.g. X 47 = X 40, 241 = 143, 145 = 69, I 118 = X 9): see "Lines shared".

### Open decisions for the user
1. *sābītu* = `בַּעֲלַת־הַמִּסְבָּאָה` (chosen; keeps the cognate root סבא), or the shorter `הַמּוֹזֶגֶת` / `הַפּוּנְדְּקָאִית`?
2. The fixed passage in plene spelling: `זיווך`, `זיווי` (the table's `זִיוְךָ` strips to "זיוך") – confirm the plene forms for all copies (and for VIII later)?
3. *matīma* = `אִי־פַּעַם` (modern-sounding) in 79 and 270, or `מֵעוֹדִי`-type periphrases (`מעולם` is reserved)?
4. *ṭabāḫu* D "flay" (261) = `מַפְשִׁיט` (natural; Lev 1:6), although פשט also renders *šaḫāṭu* "take off (a garment)" (I) – or the cognate `טבח` (not idiomatic for skins)?
5. *šibbu* (94, 165) = `חֲגוֹר` (2 Sam 20:8) – the root חגר also renders *ezēḫu* (I); alternative `מֵזַח` is taken (VII *miserru*).
6. *šumu* (301) = `שֵׁם` (chosen, cognate, ↔ Gen 6:4; 11:4) against George's "progeny" (`זַרְעָהּ` would hide the cognate).
7. *ūmīšu* (322) = `ימיו` (plural, as the manuscripts' UD-MEŠ) or George's singular `יומו` (closer to Gen 27:2 `יוֹם מוֹתִי`)?
8. *nadû* "set down (a throne)" (271) = `השליכו` (literal, corpus rule) or `הציבו` by sense?
9. *mindē-ma* (13) = `אולי` (CDA) or George's `אכן` ("for sure")?
10. 263: the restoration *lipḫû bābša* "may they seal its door" is George's; the line's Hebrew brackets it all (`[יסתמו? את שערה?]`), so the Gen 6:14 parallel rests on *kupru* only (preserved).

| 312 | nāru iššâ mīla ubla | הנהר נִשָּׂא, הביא גאות | `וַיִּרְבּוּ הַמַּיִם וַיִּשְׂאוּ אֶת הַתֵּבָה וַתָּרָם מֵעַל הָאָרֶץ` (בראשית ז:יז) | [divergence] | *našû* = נשא [cognate]; *mīlu* = the seasonal high water (`גאות`), not the deluge (*abūbu*) – the flood hero speaks of ordinary floods. |
| 313–315 | kulīlu … ultu ullânum-ma ul ibašši mimma | שפירית … ומיד – אין מאומה | `וְאֵינֶנּוּ כִּי לָקַח אֹתוֹ אֱלֹהִים` (בראשית ה:כד) | [equivalent] | Sudden disappearance ("and he was not"); [divergence] Enoch is taken, the mayfly vanishes. Also `כִּי רוּחַ עָבְרָה-בּוֹ וְאֵינֶנּוּ` (תהלים קג:טז). |
| 317 | ša mūti ul iṣṣirū ṣalamšu | של המוות – לא צרו את צלמו | `וַיִּבְרָא אֱלֹהִים אֶת הָאָדָם בְּצַלְמוֹ בְּצֶלֶם אֱלֹהִים בָּרָא אֹתוֹ` (בראשית א:כז) | [cognate] | **Key.** *ṣalmu* = צלם: man has an "image", death has none. Also `וַיּוֹלֶד בִּדְמוּתוֹ כְּצַלְמוֹ` (בראשית ה:ג). |
| 317 | iṣṣirū | צרו | `וַיִּיצֶר יְהוָה אֱלֹהִים אֶת הָאָדָם` (בראשית ב:ז) | [cognate] | *eṣēru* ~ יצר (rendered `צָר`): with *ṣalmu*, both words of Gen 1:27 and 2:7 in one line, as I 49 (Gilgameš's creation) – here negated, of death. |

| 319 | anunnakū ilū rabûtu paḫrū | האֲנוּנָּכִּי, האלים הגדולים, התקבצו | `וַיֹּאמֶר יְהוָה אֱלֹהִים הֵן הָאָדָם הָיָה כְּאַחַד מִמֶּנּוּ` (בראשית ג:כב) | [equivalent] | A divine council fixing the limit of human life; [divergence] not in response to a human act. |
| 320 | bānât šīmti | בונת הגורל | `וַיִּבֶן יְהוָה אֱלֹהִים אֶת הַצֵּלָע` (בראשית ב:כב) | [equivalent] | *banû* = בנה (never ברא/יצר): fate is "built", as Aruru "built" Enkidu (I 101); [absent] no goddess of fate in Genesis. |
| 320 | šīmatu išīm | גורל גזרה | `כִּי בְּיוֹם אֲכָלְךָ מִמֶּנּוּ מוֹת תָּמוּת` (בראשית ב:יז) | [equivalent] | The decree of death; *šīmta šâmu* = גזר גורל. |
| 321 | ištaknū mūta u balāṭa | שׂמו מוות וחיים | `רְאֵה נָתַתִּי לְפָנֶיךָ הַיּוֹם אֶת הַחַיִּים וְאֶת הַטּוֹב וְאֶת הַמָּוֶת וְאֶת הָרָע` (דברים ל:טו) | [equivalent] | **Key.** Life and death "set" before / for man; [divergence] death first, no choice offered (Deut: life first, "choose life"). *šakānu* = שׂם (reserved). OB VA+BM a+18–20: "for mankind they set death, life they kept in their own hands". |
| 321 | mūta u balāṭa | מוות וחיים | `הַחַיִּים וְהַמָּוֶת נָתַתִּי לְפָנֶיךָ הַבְּרָכָה וְהַקְּלָלָה וּבָחַרְתָּ בַּחַיִּים` (דברים ל:יט) | [equivalent] | The same pair; the order reversed. |
| 321 | mūta u balāṭa | מוות וחיים | `וְלָקַח גַּם מֵעֵץ הַחַיִּים וְאָכַל וָחַי לְעֹלָם` (בראשית ג:כב) | [equivalent] | Life withheld by the gods (explicit only in OB VA+BM a+20). |
| 321 | ištaknū | שׂמו | `מִי שָׂם פֶּה לָאָדָם` (שמות ד:יא) | [equivalent] | *šakānu* = שׂם, the creation-corpus verbal pair. |
| 322 | ša mūti ul uddû ūmīšu | של המוות – לא הודיעו את ימיו | `וַיֹּאמֶר הִנֵּה נָא זָקַנְתִּי לֹא יָדַעְתִּי יוֹם מוֹתִי` (בראשית כז:ב) | [cognate] | **Key.** *edû* = ידע (D *uddû* = הודיע): the day of death is not known (Isaac). |
| 322 | ul uddû ūmīšu | לא הודיעו את ימיו | `הוֹדִיעֵנִי יְהוָה קִצִּי וּמִדַּת יָמַי מַה-הִיא` (תהלים לט:ה) | [cognate] | Same verb (Hiphil = D) and object ("my days"): the psalmist asks for what the gods withheld. |
| 322 | ul uddû | לא הודיעו | `כִּי גַּם לֹא יֵדַע הָאָדָם אֶת עִתּוֹ` (קהלת ט:יב) | [cognate] | Man does not know his time. |

### Lines shared with other tablets
- **The fixed passage §3.2**: 40–71 = 113–148 = 213–248 (vocative and speaker only; each with its own brackets). Plene spelling: decision 3. 42/115/215 = I 118 in the 2nd person with `(למה)`; 43/116/216 = I 119 in the singular.
- **X 8–9 ~ I 118–119**: `יש יגון ב[מעיו],` / `להולך דרך רחוקה משולים פניו.` (singular *urḫi rūqati*, as the fixed passage; I 119 has the plural `דרכים רחוקות`).
- **X 92, 163 = XI 287**: `גילגמש – כשמעו את זאת,` (163 `[כשמעו]`); also binding for VIII 211, IX 136.
- **X 169–170 = XI 271–272**: `גילגמש ואורשנבי רכבו על [הספינה],` / `את הסירה השליכו (למים), הם – [רכבו] (עליה).` (own brackets).
- **X 212, 266 = XI 8** (`אותנפישתי דיבר אליו, אל גילגמש:`, own brackets).
- **Fixed here for tablets not yet translated**: VIII 50–54 (= X 53–54, 31–34 / 128–131); VIII 16–17 (animal names of 259–260: `דֹּב, צָבוֹעַ, אַרְיֵה, נָמֵר, *בַּרְדְּלָס*`, `אַיָּל, יָעֵל`); IX 5 (= 62); IX 15–17 (= 93–94, 96); IX 49 (~ 7, `בשר אֵלִים`); IX 126 (= 44/51 type); IV 4, 37, 82, 123 (~ 171 `מהלך ירח וחצי – ביום השלישי …`); OB VA+BM a+1–a+10 (~ 55–60), a+39–a+41 (~ 76, 78–79), b+1 (~ 106), b+7 (~ 109), b+21–b+28 (~ 155–161), a+18–a+20 (~ 321: `שׂמו … מוות`).
- **`verify.py` differences (62), all justified**: (a) eBL's "=" between a line and its negated or vocative counterpart in the refrain (40/47, 41/48, 42/49, 43/50, 44/51, 45/52, 113/120 … 218/225, 224/125, 51/42, 51/122, 122/115) – different Akkadian (*lā*, person); (b) eBL pairs shifted by one line (I 118 = X 9/42/49/115/122/215/222 and I 119 = X 10/43/…: the true pairs are I 118 ~ X 8, I 119 ~ X 9; X 99/164, 110/164, 145/69, 245/144, 246/144, 241/143, 243/64, 63/242, 65/240, 240/142, 242/140) – unrelated lines; (c) with or without the name Enkidu (126/127, 127/53, 133/55, 233/55, 227/226) or the second *alâ* (32/129, 32/229) or a different person/gender (32/39, 74/151, 152/75, 106/157) or addressee (46/119) – the Akkadian differs; (d) the opening quotation mark only (40/113, 40/213, 120/213, 121/214, 214/221, 215/222 …, listed under "brackets/marks only" when the brackets are the only difference).

### Proposals for the shared renderings
1. **Names / epithets (§1):** *sābītu* = `בַּעֲלַת־הַמִּסְבָּאָה` (always with maqaf; vocative too); Mammītum always vocalized `מַמִּיתוּם` (add to §7 "always vocalize": unvocalized it reads "killing them"); `אָנוּ`, `סִין` vocalized (unvocalized `אנו` = "we", `סין` = "China"); `שֵׁב` (imperative of ישב) vocalized wherever *târu*'s `שב` could be read.
2. **Key terms (§4):** *mê mūti* = `מֵי הַמָּוֶת` (confirm for the corpus); *šūt abnī* = `אַנְשֵׁי הָאֶבֶן` (used); *qinna qanānu* = `מקננים קן` (X 309 – done); *ḫimētu* = `חֶמְאָה` [cognate]; *zāninu* = `מְכַלְכֵּל`; *ṣalmu* + *eṣēru* (X 317) added to the Gen 1:27 / 2:7 rows; *šumu* "name/progeny" = `שֵׁם` always; *kupru u ittû* = `כֹּפֶר וְחֵמָר` (flood XI 66–67, X 263).
3. **Ordinary words (§5):** *matīma* = `אִי־פַּעַם`, *im-matīma* = `פַּעַם`; *edēlu* = `נָעַל`; *nagāšu* = `תָּעָה`; *kannu* = `כַּן`; *sapannu* = `שְׁפֵלָה`; *awû* Št = `שָׂח`; *zuqtu* = `סַנְטֵר`; *šaqû* D = `הִגְבִּיהַּ`; *šalāpu* = `שָׁלַף`; *šibbu* = `חֲגוֹר` (open decision 5); *ḫalālu* = `הִתְגַּנֵּב`; *šiltāḫu* = `חֵץ`; *šapû* (noise) = `הִתְעַבָּה`; *raʾābu* = `רָעַד`, *šâbu* = `רָטַט`; *ḫepû* D = `בִּקַּע`; *tabāku* (stones into a river) = `יָצַק`; *irtu* = `חָזֶה`; *urnu* = `אֹרֶן` [cognate]; *qatāpu* = `קָטַף` [cognate]; *pašāqu* = `צַר`, Š `מוּצָר`; *parāku* = `חָסַם`; *nēbertu* = `מַעְבָּרָה` (≠ *nēberu* `מַעְבָּר`); *kapāru* = `קִצֵּעַ`; *tulû* = `דַּד`; *nindanu* (measure) = `קָנֶה`; *ḫamāṣu* = `תָּלַשׁ`; *karû* (mast) = `תּוֹרֶן`; *qablu* "hips" = `מָתְנַיִם`; *šapattu* = "half-month" (by sense, `חֲצִי`; false friend `שַׁבָּת`); *etēqu* = `חָלַף`; *šerʾānu* = `גִּיד`; *nazāqu* Š = `יִסֵּר`; *kašāru* = `הִשִּׂיג`; *asu* = `דֹּב`; *mindinu* = `*בַּרְדְּלָס*`; *ṭabāḫu* D "flay" = `הִפְשִׁיט` (open decision 4); *mēlulu* = `מָחוֹל`; *lillu* = `כְּסִיל`; *šuršummu* = `שְׁמָרִים`; *tuḫḫu* = `סֻבִּין`; *nēbeḫu* = `אַבְנֵט` (used); *kapādu* = `חָשַׁב`; *tappûtu* = `חֲבֵרוּת`; *ištartu* = `אֵלָה` (vocalized `הָאֵלוֹת`); *qīštu* = `תְּשׁוּרָה`; *apu* = `אֲגַם`; *ḫaṣābu* = `גָּדַע`; *šalālu* = `שָׁלַל`, *šallu* = `שָׁלוּל`; *zērūtu* = `שִׂנְאָה`; *kulīlu* = `שַׁפִּירִית`; *neqelpû* = `צָף`; *ullânumma* = `מִיָּד`.
4. **Formula (§2):** `X – כשמעו את זאת,` confirmed (X 92, 163); *mindē-ma* = `אולי` (open decision 9).
5. **verify.py:** add `ṭīdiš` to the allowed lemmas of the `טיט` check (X 68–69, 145–146, 245–246 are the fixed passage's *ṭiṭṭiš emû*).

### Lessons for the guidelines
- **A fixed passage written vocalized in the renderings table must be given in plene spelling too**, or each translator converts it differently (`זִיוְךָ` → "זיוך" or "זיווך"); the table should carry the plene string that the `>` lines must match, and the check should compare consonants after the strip test.
- **eBL's `Parallels:` field in a threefold refrain pairs lines with their negated or neighbouring counterparts** ("=" between X 40 and X 47, X 145 and X 69): compare the normalizations before treating a listed pair as identical, and justify every reported difference in the tablet's "Lines shared".
- **A reserved-word check keyed to lemmas must list every lemma form of the word** (here *ṭīdiš*, the adverb of *ṭīdu*), or the fixed passage itself fails the check.
- **Generating the annotated and clean files from one data file** (the eBL normalization inserted by script) removed every transcription and synchronization error for a 325-line tablet.
